### 2026-11-18 — [ARCHITECTURE][SMART DATA][USER DESIGN REVIEW] BK-20261118-ARCH-CENTRAL-GATEWAY — CENTRAL DHAN DATA PIPELINE, PROPOSAL ONLY
- **USER QUESTION / WHY:** Owner asks whether the BOT KING data-management design should expose an always-available Dhan→Oracle pipeline, with BOT KING managing chosen symbols/timeframes/refresh so server edits are infrequent. Request is conceptual architecture evaluation and does NOT itself verify Dhan WebSocket entitlements or authorize an uncontrolled live deployment.
- **SOURCE EVIDENCE:** Reviewed GitHub docs/BOT_KING_REFRESH_DEMAND_INVENTORY_20261116.md and docs/BOT_KING_ORACLE_APP_CONTRACT_20261115.md. Current read-only Android/Oracle have selected 0–70 equity quote route, three independent index quotes, app polls/TTL, server caching plus some single-flight/429 controls, but fully Oracle-owned continuously collected read-only quote/candle/options revision pipeline and central demand coordination are not proven deployed. Repo backend code differs from production /opt/bot-king-server/server.py; NEVER replace production wholesale.
- **OFFICIAL DHAN DOC REVIEW (as of 2026-11-18):** https://dhanhq.co/docs/v2/market-quote/ says up to 1000 instruments in a quote REST request, 1 request/second; https://dhanhq.co/docs/v2/live-market-feed/ publishes WebSocket subscription support with limits (5 connections/user, 5000 instruments/connection). Dhan streaming packets are event-driven, not a guaranteed one-new-price-per-second per symbol. Option chain and history have different REST pacing and entitlement needs. Source availability does NOT mean unlimited per-second API calls.
- **PROPOSED BEST TARGET:** ONE Dhan authenticated connection/collector owner in Oracle (WebSocket market feed when entitlements and correctness verified; REST quote fallback only within documented bounds), one versioned per-symbol shared read-only cache (LTP, timestamp origin, received/collection age, stale/last-good; 1m/3m/5m candle derivation only from valid evidence), separate 3-index subscription/priority and selected 0/5/51/70 equity set; separate history/expiry/options OI/PCR/max-pain collectors under 429/422, TTL, singleflight; normalized event revisions over authenticated Android↔Oracle stream or conditional delta endpoint, durable reconnect/snapshot resume. BOT KING mobile sends desired saved/visible symbols, timeframe, UI/filter demand; decides local priority and render cadence, NEVER opens direct uncontrolled Dhan fanout. Server enforces entitlements, auth, quota, shared cache across 1 or 2 phones; dynamic subscriptions/refcount and revision fanout must dedup user-owned symbols and preserve cache for brief disconnect.
- **DESIGN TRADEOFF / IMPORTANT CORRECTION:** An open pipeline should be an ALWAYS REACHABLE AUTHENTICATED GATEWAY, not an unlimited Dhan proxy. Oracle owns upstream collection and broker protection; BOT KING owns UI priorities, selection and data-only refresh. A local 1–2 second render observation never promises new exchange data. News is distinct RSS, futures/options feeds must be independently validated, five scoring filters never invent signals, sixth filter remains blank. Keep market 09:10–15:45 IST auto window and after-hours manual data-only refresh; WebSocket keepalive/reconnect should be separated from allowed market-data polling rules.
- **RELEASE PLAN / GATES:** No broad server rewrite before finishing current app source CI. (1) Collect read-only Oracle runtime evidence for current WebSocket permission, Dhan entitlement, existing collector routes, CPU/RAM/memory, currently deployed SHAs/rate-limit, and baseline request count/candle staleness. (2) Implement staged isolated gateway module, behind safe feature flag with existing REST unchanged. (3) Prove finite offline synthetic event ingestion, late/out-of-order ticks, stale on disconnect, 0/5/51/70 unique roster + 3 indexes, two-device same-symbol dedup, 429/reconnect/backpressure, closed market and options 422, rollback. (4) Deploy with backups/hash+health+unauth403+authenticated read-only smoke only after proof, then exact-APK regression and device acceptance. No current Oracle deploy, restart, Dhan API call, order, or APK. Current recent #1954 CI was PASS; #1955 was last observed in progress, verify fresh before next code change.

[B3 FUTURE SINGLE-PIPE FAST REFRESH ARCHITECTURE REVIEW — 2026-11-21 IST]
- Owner clarified objective: replace V1's sluggish 2m/3m/5m apparent limits with the fastest stable Dhan-fed update possible; preserve independent Clone in-app settings, a shared Oracle transport/token pipe, no hangs/429/502, and paced rotating watchlist that naturally takes 60-90s rather than impossible all-symbol 20s candle refresh.
- Verified DhanHQ v2 official Market Quote supports up to 1000 instruments per REST batch with 1 request/second; general Data API 5 calls/second and up to 100000/day; live Market Feed supports WebSocket push, 100 instruments/subscription message and up to 5000 instruments/connection; option-chain unique request pacing around 3 seconds (see DhanHQ official v2 documents). Market feed packet timing depends on market updates and access; do NOT promise every instrument changes every second or claim a 1s candle interval exists. Authenticated Data API entitlement must be validated before production stream.
- Reviewed Clone current backend quote lane: serialized market_lock, 1.05s pacing, 20s per-selection cache and 60s for full validated 3-index batch; app has separate quote, scanner, history and option loops, scanner auto min 30s and off-hours pause. Clone now has 180s validated historical candle cache and singleflight, but historical requests still cannot deliver true 1s prices; 1m candle means one completed candle/minute. Existing 1-4 phones can duplicate logical UI demands if each drives fresh calls.
- Recommended next architecture, not deployed: Dhan WebSocket -> single Clone Oracle subscriber with demand-driven watchlist (3 index + selected unique equities <=70), timestamp/sequence-validated bounded last-good quote cache -> one shared authenticated push/delta or cache-read route to 1-4 Clone devices; phones can repaint visible LTP at 1s only on newly received events, never force one REST request per phone/sec. Keep all preferences/refresh priorities on phones; Oracle only transient subscription union, transport, authentication/renewal and shared immutable provider snapshots, not server-owned app configuration. REST quote batching as bounded recovery/fallback within 1req/s, no repeated full 70-symbol crawl; candle bootstrap/reconciliation separated from quote stream and no fabricated 1s candles; option/expiry distinct 3s-or-slower gated lane; 429 exponential bounded backoff, stale/unverified indicator and no fake votes.
- Suggested planning targets (must benchmark and tune, not broker guarantees): visible index/LTP UI update <=1s cadence on new packets; watchlist displayed LTP 1-5s cadence if stream works; 70-equity candle/assessment rotation over 60-90s rather than all within 20s; 1m scanner evaluated on newly completed candle, 3m Watchlist on 3m close; background off-hours quiet. No new code or Oracle deployment in this architecture discussion.

## 2026-11-21 IST — [BOT KING V3][SUDHAR 1] FIRST-LOGIN COMPLETE CAPACITY VERDICT — READ-ONLY SOURCE AUDIT
**Owner's exact scope:** Suppose all valid quote, 1m/3m/5m candles, five-filter evidence and other downstream results for **54 total symbols (51 equities + 3 indices)** or up to 70 equities are already received at Android endpoint at t=0. Determine whether present Building can VALIDATE→STOCK→COMPUTE→MOUNT/RENDER **ALL** data in 1s vs 3s vs 5s. Network and new Dhan historical requests excluded by assumption; strict actual market source validity and HIST/WAIT remain.

**Verdict:** Source cannot substantiate **1-second**, **3-second**, **5-second** or previous illustrative **3–8-second** complete first-login guarantee. No device CPU/RAM/profile timing, Flet-Python→Flutter update/paint timings, payload size, number of candle bars and actual full-data filter work per symbol have been measured. **Architecture is PARTIALLY PREPARED, not proven 1-second full-load capable.** This is a source-evidence judgement, NOT an Android stopwatch measurement of >1s.

**Actual source facts:**
- `src/main.py::fill_watch` clears all `watch_row_cells` and `watch_list.controls`, loops visible watch group, calls 3m signal analysis, five_engine_chips, assessment notes, signal and creates at least six mounted price/change/filter/assessment/signal/freshness controls per equity plus nested rows, icons and buttons; finally `page.update()`. At 51 equities, at least **306 named data controls** plus all wrappers; at 70 equities at least **420**. Actual performance must account for full complex Flutter tree, not just the number of equity quotes.
- `src/main.py::refresh_mounted_data_sections` builds **six Dashboard sections** or **three Scanner sections** with `host.content=factory();host.update()` for EVERY refresh, even if only one section actually changed; mounted outer page helps navigation but does NOT provide per-widget complete data delta.
- `src/main.py::watch_visible_tick` runs every 2s, computes `tick_signals={sym:watch_three_minute_signal(sym) for sym in visible}` on each tick and creates per-symbol signatures. Prior S1 fix conditionally avoids rebuilding some evidence child controls when only a quote changed; it does not prove a full first render <1s or eliminate all repeated compute.
- `src/main.py::shared_history` and `get_market_data` provide separate generation/timeframe historical caches and local completed 3m derivation from 1m; filters enforce source/age/quality. `src/clone_day2_apply_queue.py::SmartApplyQueue` is **isolated/not imported or wired into production app** and its `apply_tick` is max-items count based, NOT actual elapsed time or measured per-frame budget. No unified first-login BUFFER→COMPUTE→BUDGETED UI apply pipeline or complete per-stage Help Doctor timing is implemented.
- Existing CI 1438 offline unit tests and S1 signed diagnostic APK build validate source contracts/build but **do not benchmark 54/70-symbol full UI rendering**. Actual phone screenshots S1 show 49/51 HIST and SENSEX quote/candle failure, so do not imply all evidence is present and verified. Real 1-second server payload is only a hypothetical input.

**External design references checked:** Flet async-app guide https://flet.dev/docs/cookbook/async-apps/ documents asynchronous callbacks and control.update; Flutter official performance guide https://docs.flutter.dev/perf/best-practices advises minimizing repeated expensive builds, and Flutter DevTools https://docs.flutter.dev/tools/devtools/performance says >16ms frame induces visible jank (60Hz). These are framework principles, **not V3 phone measurements**.

**Acceptance/profiling contract before claiming any T:** Generate deterministic synthetic complete 51+3 and 70+3 symbol quote/candle/filters with fixed timestamps, valid per-timeframe completed histories and configured five filters; measure separately `t0 android payload received`, `t1 decoded+validated`, `t2 bounded stock committed`, `t3 all required filters/timeframes computed`, `t4 visible controls mounted+submitted`, `t5 Flutter rendered and interactive`; include cold vs warm, CPU peak/RAM, p50/p95/p99, 0 lost candle closes, 0 stale cross-timeframe or false LIVE and scrolling during load. Segment results: FULL STOCK READY != ALL CALCULATED != FIRST SCREEN USABLE != ALL SCREENS RENDERED. Test 10/35/51/70 equities and 1–4 devices without broker requests first; record actual latency to classify <1s, 1–3s, 3–5s or >5s.

**Recommended V3 B1 Building repair:** staged first login: verify source data once; stock immutable last-good generation/symbol/timeframe/expiry, compute results off UI where safe, prioritize 3 index dashboard/selected Watchlist first, preserve all data for offscreen views and mount lazily; bounded queue with tiny dirty UI batches and actual phone frame budget, keep only latest replaceable LTP deltas but never lose completed candles. B2 timeframes must remain strictly isolated, B3 sequential UI update must be measured; final separate gated Test APK and Help Doctor stage timings. **NO code mutation, broker API call, server deployment, synthetic benchmark, CI or APK build performed by this read-only review.**


## 2026-11-21 IST — [BOT KING V3][SUDHAR 1] FIRST-LOGIN 1/3/5s SYNTHETIC QUEUE STRESS — READ-ONLY MODEL, NOT ANDROID

**Operator requirement:** Before changing V3 Building/Timeframe or producing another APK, inject a hypothetical complete already-received data burst into a model of current `src/clone_day2_apply_queue.py` and assess 1/3/5s full first-login processing. No Dhan/Oracle/network request; no app code or APK was changed.

**Source snapshot:** Clone branch `pilot/v1-pure-original-clone-phone-20261120`: `SmartApplyQueue(max_pending=512)`, `pending` key=(section,symbol,kind,timeframe,expiry), `receive` rejects a NEW key if at capacity, `apply_tick(max_items)` selects by static priority and count; it neither computes actual five filters nor invokes Flutter UI or measures elapsed render time. Completed candle bool is stored in `Update` but `receive` does not prevent a later higher-revision candle with same key from superseding an unapplied completed candle. This is a potential loss of mandatory candle-close event if integration incorrectly uses the same coalescing lane.

**SIMULATION METHOD, NOT PRODUCTION ACTUAL EXECUTION:** Created a separate local Python queue model aligned to the source `receive`/capacity/sort/apply contract; synthetic **10 updates per instrument** = 1 LTP quote + 3 candle timeframes (1m,3m,5m) + 5 filter results + 1 assessment summary. It does NOT include extra option-chain rows, multiple screens per symbol, nested Flutter controls, source validation costs or actual candle-compute CPU, so it is only a representative initial burst, **not a measurement of complete BOT KING**. Feed all updates at t0, assume 60Hz, at most 16ms/frame, cost per applied item hypothetically 1,3,5,10ms, enforce fixed budget 1s/3s/5s, priority and cap 512. Source `src/main.py::fill_watch` independently builds 6 named mounted cells per equity and final page.update; dashboard/scanner still rebuild 6/3 sections; `SmartApplyQueue` NOT integrated into current Android. Model CSV stored as local generated `/mnt/data/v3_capacity_simulation.csv`, NOT installed or downloaded from CI.

**Model outcome when 54 total instruments (51 equities + 3 indices):** 540 distinct keys, **512 admitted and 28 REJECTED** regardless of delay. Under hypothetical 3ms/item and 16ms/frame: 1s applies 300/540 with 240 outstanding; 3s and 5s each apply 512/540 with 28 permanently rejected (unless producer retries). At 5ms/item: 1s applies 180/540, 3s/5s 512/540. At 10ms/item: 1s 60/540, 3s 180/540, 5s 300/540 (plus 28 rejected).
**Model 73 instruments (70 equities + 3 indices):** 730 distinct keys, **512 admitted, 218 rejected**. Under assumed 3ms/item 1s 300/730, 3s/5s 512/730, so 218 still unprocessed without explicit retry.
**Model 10 and 35** also generated in CSV; no real Flutter runtime timing inferred.

**CRITICAL CONCLUSION:** Current isolated SmartApplyQueue **does not safely handle this illustrative complete 54/73-instrument × 10-stream simultaneous burst without backpressure recovery**. Even with arbitrarily fast render per update, 512 cap rejects required keys. Consequently a source-free declaration of 1/3/5s FULL first-login success would be false. This is a *queue-model bottleneck*, not proof that actual Android V3 currently drops those 28/218 items, because prototype is NOT wired into V3; likewise these timings are hypothetical item-cost budgets, NOT phone p50/p95. Actual first login currently uses its own UI/network pathways and requires profiling.

**Correct B1 next test/repair policy:** bounded authoritative last-good stock separate from disposable UI apply queue (incoming valid records never lost when queue full), lazy visible-first materialization, per-key generation/timeframe/expiry and completeness, separate lossless completed candle commit from coalescible quote/UI hints, adaptive dirty-set and producer backpressure/retry, off-main-thread compute, explicit T0 receive/T1 validate/T2 cache/T3 compute/T4 mounted submit/T5 Flutter first/last paint timing; test 10/35/51+3/70+3 and 1–4 phones with offline synthetic complete data; conditional 1/3/5s acceptance only on Android traces. B2 strict timeframe and B3 display/queue integration after building. **No source fix, workflow, APK or server change made in this diagnosis.**
